Every gen-5 literal pinned in the tests held, as the flip leaves the JAX engine on gen 5 (gen 7 is the mounted render). Tests on disk: test_sa_granted_actor.py and test_sa_scratchpad.py 27 passed, test_sa_archery.py 5 passed. Gate: the package command with HYTALE_ASSETS_ZIP on the working tree at b774422 with this change: 1225 passed, 1 failed, 2 skipped, 1 xfailed in 26 min (1222 at ff3415d; the four are this change's tests: remembered avoid, the two archery tests and position_used). The coordinator's uncommitted tasks/duel_arena.py edit was in the tree and is not part of this change.
verdict: BUILT and MEASURED. A request's position is no longer ignored silently: the decision layer reads `position_used` beside `seen`. The coordinator appends the name to `REPORT_NAMES` (their test pins the five names until then) and handles the trace refusal with the flip. No training.

## 2026-10-20 — coordinator: the policy distribution's body-look and hotbar heads were unnormalized in training (4cc2490, F6)
command: reported from code by the skill layer's action-space survey; verified by the coordinator (shift each head's logits by a constant and compare the log-probability); fixed and tested by a Haiku lane (the distribution's own tests, a mutation run restoring the old tuple, the multi-actor rollout tests, the identity gate); swap-plan dry run after the commit.
result: before the fix, shifting body_yaw_delta_bins' or hotbar_none_plus_slots' logits by +3 moved the log-probability by exactly +3 (every other head 0), and their entropy was missing; both heads are sampled unconstrained, so they belong in _NON_ROOT_HEADS. After: shift invariance holds for all 12 heads, the joint sums to one over an enumerated support, exp(log-prob) equals the product of the two heads' softmax (20k sampler draws agree), and each head adds its own entropy; 18 new tests, 7 of which fail with the old tuple. The rollout test that forces a one-hot action and expects log-prob 0 failed before (−0.000124) and passes now. Sampling and greedy selection are unchanged. The swap plan needs no change (0 line moves).
verdict: FIXED. Any PPO through jax/training/policy.py (ppo.py's new-policy log-prob and entropy bonus; the multi-actor rollout's recorded log-prob) trained these two heads on an unnormalized score since the initial release; log-probabilities recorded before 4cc2490 must not be mixed with ones scored after it. ## 2026-10-21 — skill_agent: where the memory's time goes, and a proposed design (VIRTUAL_HUMAN.md section 18, PROPOSED)
command: owner: "the goal is that the latent memory is quick retrieval and efficient not slow ... during fast throughput areas it is slower ... store entities and important things differently than normal latent blocks".
result: Measured (synthetic rows, load 7-8, median ms per tick): writing the stores grows with the rows seen a tick and the store's size (batch 8: 2.0 at 84 slots and 44 rows, 3.7 at 88 rows, 6.4 at 336 slots; batch 64: 6.2, 11.2, 31.0), reading them 0.1-2.3, the trunk 4.1 (batch 8) and 22.5 (batch 64), 8 to 32 latent slots adding 0.3-1.1. The cause is the serial one-row-at-a-time upsert copying the whole store each step (`state/memory.py`). A parallel prototype (scratch) is 5-10x faster (1.63 to 0.26 ms; 32.3 to 4.5 ms). Remembered terrain as 84 trunk tokens costs about a fifth of the trunk (348 to 280 tokens: 3.9 to 3.1 ms at batch 8, 19.4 to 15.9 at batch 64). Proposed: object files for important things (identity-keyed, exact, own capacity, one latent slot each) and a gist for the background (16 latent summary slots, Perceiver-style), with a parallel, budgeted write; research checked by search (Kahneman, Treisman and Gibbs 1992; Oliva and Torralba 2006; Hydra 2022; Perceiver 2021; Neural Map 2018).
verdict: COMMITTED (docs; nothing built); not pushed. Four decisions wait on the owner (section 18.3).

Pass 2 (0.5.7, `-m stand_in_pursuit`): 19 failed and 11 errors on both, the same names. With the new parts off, the trunk is bit-identical to before (scratch check against the old module). Timing on real Region rows (gen 7 with the perception channel), each side run from its own worktree (88a2404 against c3fc181 with this diff), median: the whole tick at batch 8, 6.2 to 3.9-4.7 ms; at batch 64, 21.6-22.2 to 15.2-15.8 ms; the store write 6 to 8 times faster; E8 p95 4.42 ms at batch 8.
What it adds: (1) `state/memory.py`: a parallel one-pass write (equal to the serial write slot for slot when there is room; when candidates compete, the later twin wins, refreshed and pinned slots are kept, the most salient new ones are written); `Store.latent` (one latent per stored entity and block candidate, keyed by identity) and `object_slots`. (4) `state/layer.py`: `AgentState.object_slots` and `background`; a save from before loads with the new parts at their starting values. (5) `runtime/state_tick.py` carries the background and gathers and scatters the object latents.
Corrected: section 18.1's "a busy place costs more": every shape is fixed, so the write's cost follows its capacity and the store's size, not what is seen. A first before/after timing that imported the new code on both sides (`python -m` from the main tree shadows a worktree on PYTHONPATH) is withdrawn (18.4).

result: `state/written.py` (new; a Haiku subagent built it from a written spec, reviewed and re-run here): a typed `Write` (writer, field, value, an entity id or a world position) checked on the host against the registry and refused with a reason (unknown field, wrong writer, out of range, bad key, two keys, too many writes this tick); `apply` at the start of each tick, up to 8 writes per agent in order into 16 entity and 16 place entries, stamped with writer and tick; never fades, never evicts (a write to a full table is refused, `Written.applied`), ejected by writing the defaults; the stores' tags are derived from the live `remember` entries. `contract/variables.py`: `WRITERS`, `Variable.writer`, `ENTITY_ENTRIES` and `PLACE_ENTRIES` (`remember`, [0, 1], the decision layer). `state/layer.py`: `step(..., writes=)` replaces `tags=`; save and load carry the entries and migrate their fields by name. `state/memory.py`: the placeholder write for unseen tagged places runs only when one exists (a `lax.cond`), which removed the extra cost of passing tags every tick (stores' update at batch 64: 0.83 ms with the derived tags before the fix, 0.61 to 0.65 ms without tags). ## 2026-10-24 — skill_agent: training through time, the sequence machinery (`skill_agent/docs/TRAINING_PLAN.md` 8.7; no training)
command: owner, 2026-10-23: "Start V1 smoke (Recommended)" (the smoke needs this machinery first; TRAINING_PLAN.md 8.2 item 2).
result: `training/sequences.py` (record, cut, step, unroll, pack, unpack) and `runtime/state_tick.trunk_step` (`tick`'s trunk half moved out unchanged), built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec and checked here. On a 12-decision, 2-lane flat-world rollout with a fresh v3 trunk (L = 6, a reset mid-lane), replaying each stored sequence one decision at a time reproduces every trunk output bit for bit; the batched `lax.scan` is within 1.91e-6 (float32 rounding across program shapes: the jitted rollout differs from the eager one by the same 1.91e-6, re-run here), so the scan test's bound is 1e-5. float16 storage: at most 4.65e-4. Gradients reach the memory's initial slots through time. The restart mask is exact under changed parameters (the state layer only keeps or zeroes a slot's latent, `state/memory.py` line 184). With `ModuleBody` as shipped (its own request only), looking while walking falls to 0.781 held out (reference 0.906).
- **For the skill layer** (sent by message; recorded here as well):
  1. `ModuleBody` should put the agent's other live requests of the verbs its module knows into the request slots (as `distill._request_slots` trained it; `neural_skills/spec.py`: a module reads the other active requests of skills it was trained beside). Today a body sees only its own; held out, looking while walking loses 5 of 32 lanes. A body needs the agent's live requests in its context for that (the harness passes `(AgentView, live requests)`); the combiner side of that is the coordinator's, the body side yours.
  2. `own_pose_channel` builds a new NamedTuple class on every call; a host caller outside jit makes the compiled `step_lanes` retrace every tick (6.5 s a tick measured). Caching the class in `own_pose_channel` fixes it for every caller.
  3. A `ModuleBody` never reports done, so a goal the decision layer ends as achieved shows in the status stream as `aborted` ("withdrawn"). The generalist has a trained done readout (`module_outputs(...).done`); using it, or a protocol "withdrawn as complete", would keep successes out of the abort count.
  4. Host cost: 3 to 5 ms per agent per tick at 96 agents (bodies at batch 1 per agent and verb). Batching a module across agents per tick is the path to 30 Hz with many agents.
- **Coordinator follow-ups:** `session.run_session` cannot drive a host `ModuleBody` (its in-graph actor runs array bodies only) and runs the state tick after the combiner (bodies would read the previous tick's view; at most one lane here). A host-body mode for the session is next on this side.
- **Fixes landed today from 6413a52's findings:** (4) dbdb7ed, `import hytalegym` loaded 353 hytalegym.jax modules through `envs/block_type_table.py` since 6d9307f, so the gen-7 launcher's `switch_on` came too late and no construct reached the observation; the import is deferred and `tests/sim/test_import_order.py` guards it. (2) ec8c28b, the hearing tables are built under `jax.ensure_compile_time_eval`. (1, 3) in progress: the duel's constructs (done in the duel arena with the hooked encoder per seat, so the gen-7 render stays `combat/` only) and an opt-in `floor_block` for a stepped world that makes MoveIn sounds.
- **Also:** 4b6c01f, a held charge reports its slot as active from the press (row 5f); a gen-7 learner stamina fix is in its gate (the bridge leases remote-client mode for policy chains, so the live learner pays an instant ChangeStat once: sword thrust 10 to 6 live, the render 8; autonomous NPCs still pay twice).
gate: `run_architecture.py` ruff clean; smoke and four full runs above. dbdb7ed: 39 sim + 6 adk tests; ec8c28b: 41; 4b6c01f: tests/sim 638 passed, 8 skipped, 4 xfailed (baseline 633/8/3).
verdict: COMMITTED; not pushed.

## 2026-10-25 — coordinator: speed asks answered (render cache, Region clock), duel constructs, learner costs, quick-press root, recertification 13
command: owner, 2026-10-25: "note to also do optimiatons and icnrease speed of existing training and models when possible"; the skill layer's four speed asks and its Region clock question (messages; the coordinator's reply about the cache and the clock expired undelivered, so it is recorded here).
result:
- **Render cache (704151d):** `hytalegym.sim.common.render.render_cached(overlay, source=HeadSource(), cache_root=None) -> Path`. Key: the PACKAGE_ROOT git tree at the commit, `overlay.sha256()`, the profile record, the working tree's `hytalegym/sim` files (render outputs excluded) and the Assets.zip identity. Location `HYTALEGYM_RENDER_CACHE` or `~/.cache/hytalegym/renders`, written atomically, read-only to callers (`mount` reads it in place), newest 24 kept. Cold 37.5 s, warm 0.26 s under load 136; byte-identical to a fresh render (`tests/sim/test_render_cache.py`). `runtime/gen7.py --render` can call it and mount the returned path.
- **Region clock "pause" (not a clock bug):** the stalled lanes had died. `can_tick` is gated on `~_arsenal_stopped` (`jax/combat/arsenal/runtime.py:1096-1101`) and `tick_count` advances only inside it (:1105), so a finished lane freezes, clock included, until the caller resets it. Repro: batch 2, 300 random legal steps; each stall began the step after that lane's first `done`, failure bits 0 (coordinator's tmp/lane_clock_pause/FINDINGS.md). The integration check should count clock ticks only up to each lane's `done` (or reset finished lanes); the strict xfail can become "the clock advances one tick per step before done". A world clock across death belongs with the respawn design.
- **Profile of the architecture loop (your item 3):** combiner and scratchpad Python are negligible (`combiner.step` 0.8 s own time of 76 s). The cost is inside `ModuleBody.__call__` (1,674 calls, 74.7 s; 32 lanes, 60 ticks, under load): slicing the whole `StateView` per call (`state_tick.py:370` `tree_map(lambda leaf: leaf[row:row+1], view)`, about 21 ms), `device_get` of the head logits (about 14 ms), and the eager batch-1 lowerings (about 7 ms). Batching a module per verb across agents removes the first two.
- **Landed for gen 7 (affects training data):** ec7410d, the duel's seats carry perception_v2, game_clock and heard_sounds (each seat's actor-first view through the hooked encoder; the render stays `combat/`), and `make_duel_arena(floor_block="Plant_Bush")` gives a stepped world with MoveIn sounds (your findings 1 and 3). a80b471, the learner (entity 0) pays native NPC instant stat costs once (kinds 2 and 3: stamina, signature energy and charges, mana; the thrust costs 4 not 8), matching live, and a quick press on a charging slot reports the charging slot through its released child.
- **Recertification run 13** passed on jar 16 (49CDB408) and was promoted; v8 re-frozen 15DF0300 (812c2ef); stamp `combat-contract-stamp-c0124bcd`.
- **Next on the coordinator's side:** the Region fixture cache as a new opt-in wrapper module (the loader lives in `HytaleRL/examples/jax_arsenal_world_benchmark.py` and `worlds/region.py`, both with another session's uncommitted edits); per-tick host transfers in the Region/duel env.step; the gen-5 vs gen-7 perf bench on a quiet machine (armed).
gate: tests/sim 677 passed, 8 skipped, 3 xfailed for ec7410d + a80b471 (HEAD baseline 662/8/4); render cache 2 passed and render identity 4 passed.

## 2026-10-25 (later) — coordinator: speed — Region build 175 s -> 16 s, Region step LOS early exit, cached test renders, the gen-7 cost
command: owner, 2026-10-25: "note to also do optimiatons and icnrease speed of existing training and models when possible"; the skill layer's Region fixture-cache ask.
result:
- **Gen 7 costs little (measured, load 4.6-5.9):** tick step 1.06× gen 5 at batch 1 and 1.03× at batch 8; compile 4.28 s against 3.75 s, all of it from four constructs (hit_interrupt 0.36 s, guard_cancel_window 0.2, landing_walk_resume 0.15, weapon_durability 0.15; the other 38 cost nothing measurable); the learner encode's first compile per process is 0.15 s longer. Not the training-speed lever.
- **Region step (quiet profile, load 2.4-3.9, HEAD 2bd831c):** batch 32 85 ms/step, batch 8 44 ms; 87% inside the world geometry lookups. Every line-of-sight call (`jax/world/perception/los.py` `_geometry_line_of_sight_result`) runs a fixed `fori_loop` of 128 iterations (the Region capacity, `ceil(sqrt(3) * distance) + 2`), and each iteration does the block lookups of `region/atlas.py` and `region/mutable_geometry.py` for all rows. Measured iterations until every row is done (Region batch 32, 50 steps, neutral actions): token candidates median 1, max 6; perception and hitbox pairs median 2, max 4.
- **LOS early exit (18fec74):** opt-in `HYTALEGYM_LOS_EARLY_EXIT=1` replaces the loop with a `while_loop` that stops when every row is done; a done row is a fixed point of `visit`, so the outputs are identical. Bit-exact on Region batch 8 and 32 and the duel (every carried leaf at steps 1/10/50/100, two fresh processes per arm). Region batch 8 45.1 -> 14.5 ms/step, batch 32 85.1 -> 31.8 (five alternating pairs, load mostly 2-6.5), duel batch 8 0.94 -> 0.94; compile unchanged. `while_loop` has no reverse-mode gradient; every gradient in the repository is over collected batches, none through the env step.
- **Region build (b575d01):** a clean-HEAD `region_scene` spends 163 of 180 s in `NativeRegionSnapshot.semantic_digest` (all 288 worlds audited, the library loaded twice, each snapshot hashed twice per load). Opt-in `HYTALEGYM_REGION_DIGEST_CACHE=<dir>` keys each digest by the file's SHA-256 and the hash of the modules the digest reads, and uses it only when it equals the manifest's digest. 175 s unset, 97 s cold, 16 s warm; reset and 10 steps byte-identical. The main tree's uncommitted selected-only callers (`verify_artifacts=False`, another session's) already skip most of the audit; the cache stacks with them. This answers the Region fixture-cache ask without pickling the fixture.
- **Cached test renders (48a473a):** `tests/sim/support.render_for_test` reuses `render_cached` when `HYTALEGYM_RENDER_CACHE` is set (34 modules). Full suite 43 min with a fresh cache (18 renders), against 2 h 18 min uncached earlier.
- **For the skill layer:** set `HYTALEGYM_REGION_DIGEST_CACHE` (any folder) and `HYTALEGYM_LOS_EARLY_EXIT=1` in Region runs; both are bit-exact, so receipts stay comparable. `integration_gen7.py` still rebuilds `jax.random.split(jax.random.key(tick), batch)` every tick (:148, :191; ~9% of duel host time at batch 2): a table built once gives the same keys.
- **Follow-ups:** two gitignored worldgen tests fail on unmodified HEAD (`test_region_traversal_identity.py::test_current_v3_graph_library_replays_with_separate_provenance`: recorded bridge jar 5A54A8F5 vs pin 49CDB408; `test_world_contract_artifact_references.py::test_world_contract_digest_references_match_named_artifacts`: 9 stale contract digests). The 677 recorded for ec7410d + a80b471 was two tests short of what landed; today's gate on 2bd831c counts 679, all passing.
gate: tests/sim with the LOS edit committed in a worktree (so renders read it), sharded: 684 passed, 8 skipped, 3 xfailed (= 679 + the 5 digest-cache tests), 12 min; with the switch set, test_line_of_sight, test_perception_v2, test_legal_observation_privilege and test_constructs 80/8/3. 28 tests/jax LOS files: the same 17 failures unset, set and on unmodified HEAD (506 passed). Worldgen Region tests 90/1/2 with the digest cache unset and set (the 2 fail on HEAD too). Render switch: 679/8/3 single process (43 min). New gate tool: `run_sim_sharded.py` (four pytest processes on one render cache, files balanced by a previous junit) gives the same 679/8/3 in 12.4 min.
verdict: COMMITTED (b575d01, 48a473a, 18fec74); not pushed.

## 2026-10-25 — skill_agent: ModuleBody reads the live requests it was trained beside; trained done; the pose channel's class cached (the coordinator's architecture test, 9cff734)
command: the coordinator's architecture test (9cff734, eval/README.md): the trained walk_to and look_at bodies work end to end through the decision layer, S_t and the combiner, but looking while walking held out reaches 0.781 against the reference's 0.906, because ModuleBody gives the module only its own request.
result: Built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec, checked here. The API, for the coordinator (two messages expired today):
- **`AgentView` is unchanged** (5 fields, so the harness's unpacking still works). A new `JointView(agent, live)` carries the agent's live requests by verb name. `encode_contexts(..., live_requests={agent: {verb: request}})` returns JointViews; without it, AgentViews as before.
- **`ModuleBody(..., layout=..., report_done=...)`.**
  - `layout` is the verbs in the slot order the module was trained with: `V1_LAYOUT = ("walk_to", "look_at")` for every `v1_bodies/*` body (`distill._request_slots`), and `v2_layout(skill, beside) = (skill, *beside)` for V2 modules (`skill_dagger.build_requests`). Each slot holds its verb's request, lowered by that skill's own `lowering`, or is empty with its verb and scope set.
  - None keeps the own-request-only body.
  - `report_done=True` reports done when the module's trained done logit is above 0; the default is never done. Holding skills are never done.
- **`own_pose_channel`** caches its NamedTuple class per field set, so host callers no longer make the compiled `step_lanes` retrace every tick (it compiled 3 times over 3 ticks before; once after).
- **The coordinator's harness** (`eval/run_architecture.py`, 96 lanes, held out, seed 77001, `v1_bodies/memory_s0`), driven from a scratch script that passes `live_requests`:

| | both held | looking while walking | walking held |
|---|---|---|---|
| default | 0.750 | 0.781 | 0.969 |
| `V1_LAYOUT` | 0.875 | 0.906 | 0.969 |
| reference | 0.906 | 0.906 | 1.000 |

  - 0 illegal decisions in every arm.
  - The default arm reproduces the coordinator's numbers exactly, and looking while walking now matches the reference.
- **Tests:** `test_sa_module_body_joint.py` (local) 11 passed. Its slots equal `distill.build_requests` and `skill_dagger.build_requests` field for field; the arguments match to 1.2e-7, float32 rounding of the look direction, whose cause is unconfirmed. `test_sa_state_tick.py`, `test_sa_export_v1.py` and `test_sa_step_lanes_jit.py` pass 20 of 20, before and after.
- **V2 bodies ready:** `scratchpad/v2_bodies/memory_s1/` (V1's trunk, V2's walk_to module and look_at's 6 x 2,000 module): 32 of 32 lanes succeed and hold for both skills, seen and held out, 0 illegal. Use `v2_layout`: walk_to's module ("walk_to", "look_at"), look_at's ("look_at", "walk_to").
- **For the coordinator:** pass `live_requests` from the scratchpad's requests for each agent, and `layout=` per body. Batching a module across agents per verb (the route to 30 Hz at many agents) is noted, not built.
- **Speed** (owner, 2026-10-25: "also try to optimize or tell the other agent to optimize and increase speed"; the message may be held). Asks on the coordinator's side:
  1. Cache gen-7 renders by the rendered sources' HEAD, the constructs and the server line. Every slow test and probe renders its own engine, 30 to 85 s each.
  2. Cache Region fixtures on disk by library sha and selection. They take 19, 57 and 85 s at batch 32 for working sets of 1, 8 and 16.

result:
- **look_at at 6 x 2,000** passes every bar on both seeds: while walking, held out over seen 0.913 and 0.947 (bar 0.90), T5 +0.8 and +2.3.

    is inferred.

    planned new trunk should train on such people as well as terrain.
  - track's teacher beside walk_to holds only 0.52 and needs work.
- **The V2 bodies** drive 32 of 32 lanes for both skills, seen and held out, through ModuleBody.
- **A precision to 8.11:** its 32 + 32 check ran on the control arm. The memory arm holds 31 of 32 held-out lanes per skill, eager or compiled, within its own rates.

Runs and modules are in the session scratchpad (`v3_runs/`, `v2_bodies/`).

## 2026-10-25 — skill_agent: the trainers' terrain world, a 0.7.2 Region adapter, and the straight-line baseline (`skill_agent/neural_skills/step_and_hop/TRAINING.md`, `go_around/TRAINING.md`: "the terrain task world")
command: owner, 2026-10-24: "we want to get everything done asap so that we can start training the neural polcieis"; the next skills (step_and_hop, go_around) wait on a terrain world for the trainers.
result: Built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec, checked here.
- **`tasks/region_tasks.py`, `make_region_task_environment`:** the flat world's interface (`batch`, `reset(keys, agent_xz, agent_yaw, other_xyz)`, `step`) on the 0.7.2 Region library (`region-library-pilot-v2-072`).
  - It uses the npc_walk motion model, `legal_observation` on, entity 1 inert, and a 1/30 s tick.
  - The yaw is set after the reset.
  - Placements come from traversal pairs inside a separation band, with each lane's floor height.
  - It draws from 8 worlds per batch (`WORKING_SET_CAPACITY`): 17 distinct spawns per 32 lanes, against 2 from one world.
  - The agent's per-tick motion equals the flat world's tick for tick: 0.011, 0.022, 0.033, 0.044, then 0.050 blocks a tick.
  - The state layer fills geometry rows on every lane.
- **The straight-line baseline** (`bench/region_baseline.py`; walk_to v3's teacher, which does not path-plan; 20 s, batch 32): it reaches the goal on 66%, 47% and 28% of lanes at 3 to 6, 6 to 10 and 10 to 15 blocks (median 1.2, 2.7 and 4.8 s; 21, 19 and 16 distinct spawns). **It ran on the default (gen 5) engine**, not under `runtime/gen7`'s launcher (the builder's report), and gen 5 has no step-up (`sim/gen7/mechanics/auto_step_up.py:3`). So every route needing a climb was unreachable for any teacher, and the numbers understate gen 7, which steps up by 1.3 blocks, as the graph's climb edges do. Re-measuring under gen 7 comes with the terrain teachers (the owner's ruling: train on gen 7).
- **Gaps for the owners of `worlds/` and the simulator** (worked around here, not edited):
  - `load_region` builds the player motion model and no `legal_observation` (`worlds/region.py` ~568-570);
  - `REGION_PROVIDER_SEAMS` leaves out the capability and token providers (~619-627);
  - `ArsenalResetBatch` has no yaw (`arsenal/environment.py:463-476`);
  - this module uses `worlds.region`'s private helpers (`_library_manifests`, `_identity`, `loader`), and a public loader would remove that coupling.
- **The obstacle profiles** (climb, stairs, drop, detour) exist only as uncommitted edits in the main tree (`HytaleRL/examples/jax_arsenal_world_benchmark.py`, `arena/training/runs/pursuit_run.py`, last touched 2026-10-16). The owner (2026-10-25) said to finish and land them, and they are sim side, so a tested patch is ready for the coordinator: `scratchpad/drafts/spawn_pair_profiles.patch`.
  - It touches one file, the benchmark, +261/-59 on blob 8398e00.
  - The default "level" placements are byte-identical to HEAD.
  - Every profile's rise rule holds on the 0.7.2 library (climb 1 to 9, stairs 2 to 3, drop -6 to -2, flat_detour 0 with no sightline).
  - The benchmark's tests fail on identical names before and after, all already failing at HEAD.
  - The moves that change default placements (core margins, source spread) are left out.
  The adapter passes `spawn_pair_profile` once it lands.
- **Tests:** `test_sa_region_tasks.py` (local, slow) 7 passed: placements, off-placement refusal, the tick, legal observation and entity 1 inert, steady npc_walk speed on unobstructed walks, geometry rows, the walk_to share.
gate: its files are new, and only its own test imports them; that test passed (7) on ec8c28b, and again on 842180e with the four commits above (7 passed, with HYTALEGYM_LOS_EARLY_EXIT and the Region digest cache on). The rest of the tree is as gated for the commits above.
- **Terrain for a walker, as surveyed 2026-10-25:** the traversal graph per world (`worldgen/region/traversal.py`; edges walk, climb up to 1.3 and drop up to 5; costs horizontal), a jit-able A* that returns paths (`jax/world/navigation/__init__.py:211-392`), and npc_walk's jump, which rises only 0.41 blocks (`jax/combat/types.py:589-597`), so it climbs nothing.
verdict: COMMITTED; not pushed. Open for the owner: the obstacle-profile work, and whether step_and_hop and go_around train on the frozen V1 trunk, whose geometry weights are at their initial values (it never saw terrain), or on a trunk trained with terrain.

## 2026-10-25 — skill_agent: ModuleBatch, one skill module run across agents per verb (speed)
command: owner, 2026-10-25: "also try to optimize or tell the other agent to optimize and increase speed"; the coordinator's profile of the architecture loop (2bd831c): the cost is inside `ModuleBody.__call__`. Each call slices the whole StateView, about 21 ms; fetches its logits, 14 ms; and lowers its request eagerly, 7 ms.
result: Built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec, checked here.
- **`runtime/state_tick.ModuleBatch(module, trunk, lower, layout, report_done).run(view, agent_views, requests)`** runs one module for N agents.
  - It does one indexed take of their rows, one batched module pass (bucketed by 8) and one `device_get`, with the same slots as `ModuleBody.requests()` (now shared helpers).
  - `run_module_batches(...)` runs every verb.
  - `ModuleBody(batch_results=...)` returns the stored step for its agent's (row, tick); without it, the per-agent path is unchanged.
- **Equality:** V1 and V2 bodies, walk_to and look_at, N = 1, 8 and 32, plus 192 agent-verb steps at N = 96. Every choice and done equals the per-agent call, with no flips; the largest logit difference is 1.5e-5 (float32, batch shape).
- **Timing** (one flat-world tick, load about 25): both verbs 312 to 124 ms at N = 32 (2.5x), and 3,400 to 941 ms at N = 96 (3.6x).
- **What remains** is the per-agent lowering, now nearly all of the batch time (walk_to's `lowering` builds one jnp request per agent). A batched lowering in the skill folders is next.
- **For the coordinator:** the architecture loop gains once the harness runs `run_module_batches` each tick and passes `batch_results`. The per-call costs in 2bd831c (45 ms) did not reproduce here (5 ms at N = 32, load 25); the loop's own views or load may explain it.
- **Tests:** `test_sa_module_batch.py` (local) 17 passed; module_body_joint, state_tick and export_v1 as before.
gate: two passes, sharded (the same names and totals as the serial gate, validated 2026-10-25), on 99c404e with ModuleBatch, the integration clock fix and the cached render: pass 1 the step-9 baseline's 11 failing names, none new (the integration shard 7 passed, no xfail); pass 2 1 failing name, a baseline one. Landed on 7885b6c (the coordinator's jar 17; no shared files but the ledger).
verdict: COMMITTED; not pushed.

## 2026-10-25 — skill_agent: the integration check judges each lane's clock only while it lives; gen7.py renders through the cache (speed)
command: the coordinator's answer in 2bd831c: "Region clock pause (not a clock bug)". The stalled lanes had died, and a finished lane freezes until reset. Their speed note: rebuild the per-tick keys once. Their render cache landed in 704151d.
result: Built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec, checked here.

## 2026-10-25 (evening) — coordinator: bridge jar 17 (block damage age without overflow), recertification 14
command: owner, 2026-10-25: "continue working" (the queued jar-17 batch after the speed work).
result:
- **The bug (live, jar 16):** after a partial pickaxe break, `policy_world_action_capture` failed with `ArithmeticException: long overflow` (`RegionGeometryCapture.mutableBlockRow`, `Duration.toNanos()`; both seeds of `gen7_probes/break_neighbours_live_jar16_hits.json`). A server `BlockHealth()` starts stamped `Instant.EPOCH` (read from the 0.7.2 class) and a fresh world's game time starts in year 1, so the gap passes the ~292-year range of `toNanos()`. Which stamp the failing cell carried was not read (inferred: the default or another post-year-293 base).
- **The fix (jar 17, F4AA8EBA):** `damageAgeSeconds` returns 0 for a negative gap before converting, which is what the old `Math.max(0, ...)` returned wherever it did not throw; every other value is bit-for-bit the old one (`RegionGeometryCaptureDamageAgeTest`, local). Rebuilding HEAD unchanged gave 49CDB408 again (the build is reproducible); Java 489 -> 491 passed, run fresh.
- **Live (jar 17):** `break_neighbours_live_candidateF4AA8EBA.json`, seeds 570057 and 913771: no overflow; the first hit leaves the Rock_Stone standing, it reads Air from tick 32, the second hit finishes at tick 39; the Soil_Pebbles beside and above stay. Server log: no capture failures (jar 16: two), the same 0.7.2 `Reallocate` stderr lines.
- **Recertification run 14** (14:19 to 14:36, load 3.73 at launch) passed against jar 17 and was promoted with supersede (36 checks, 60 files, receipt `world-evidence-history/F4AA8EBA/publication-v12.json`); v8 re-frozen 15DF0300 -> 913720C7 (b12eec8). The drift is the bridge identity only: in a clean worktree under the jar-16 pin, jar 16's superseded traversal manifest gives 15DF0300 again with jar 17's evidence everywhere else (the pin also enters through the reference policy surface hash, so a string swap of the hash does not show it). Stamp `combat-contract-stamp-7a4338f8` at b12eec8 (content FE91CAEF and actor evidence FC928136 unchanged); the local publication test's repository pin is b12eec8 (12 passed).
- **Live break under a support-dependent block (DIFF, sim kept fail-closed):** with jar 17 the Soil_Pebbles on top of the broken Rock_Stone (parent Soil_Gravel, `Support.Down` Full) and beside it stay for about 200 ticks in both seeds (a 240-tick rerun, tmp/lane_support_drop/receipts/). Not a placement artifact: the fixture's `setBlock` resets the pebble's support nibble to 0, not deco 15 (`BlockOperations.setBlock` 605-639, as worldgen's fallback), and the break marks the 3x3x3 neighbourhood ticking (flag 256, `updateBlockArea` at 587). Why the server never drops it is not found. The sim's tier-1 rule refuses that break; "allow and keep the block" is the candidate change once the mechanism or a naturally generated case is read. FIDELITY-0.7.2.md (ax) "Open".
- **MoveIn timing (native rule read from the server):** `AudioSystems$TickMovementAudio` plays MoveIn of the new feet-cell block on a change of that cell (offset 128 `if_icmpeq 247`), then MoveOut of the old cell's set (only Water authors one), and re-plays MoveIn on a timer of uniform(0.5, 1.5) s (`BlockSoundSet` default; no asset overrides it) while the body is not idle (offset 284). Players and NPCs alike. The server never plays a walking footstep (`Walk` has one caller, seating), so footsteps on plain ground are client-side and absent from live `heard_sounds`. The sim already uses the feet cell and MoveIn only (grass is silent); only its timing (every 30th tick) differs. An opt-in construct with two per-entity leaves (the diagram_crafting pattern) is in a lane (tmp/lane_movein_timing).
- **Bows:** the gen-7 shortbow "never fires" was a replay without arrows: the catalog node `shortbow_primary_arrow` removes one `Weapon_Arrow_Crude` and suppresses the root when that fails (`runtime.py:1190-1212`, `item_programs.py:941-945`); the live probe carried one arrow (hotbar slot 1). With one arrow (as live; ten rows, tiers 0-4, both seeds) the first step matches live within 0.0002 blocks (12, 22, 35, 50, 85 blocks/s) and the health drop (4, 7, 19 for tiers 2-4) lands on the live tick in 9 of 10 rows; the sim's projectile appears 2-3 ticks after the live spawn frame (cause not found), and the sim reports the tier slot in flight where live reads 0. No engine change (the arenas already seed arrows on request). The crossbow still starts loaded (ammo 6) where live starts empty and loads on the first press; the reload root has no fall-through from the primary press yet (parked).
- **Stale worldgen test (cause found, not changed):** `test_region_traversal_identity.py::test_current_v3_graph_library_replays_with_separate_provenance` compares the 0.5.7 traversal library's recorded bridge (5A54A8F5) with `current_native_evidence_jar_sha256()`, which reads the default line (0.7.2 since a49d001). Even on 0.5.7 the line's constant is 7700D112 (879321e), so that library was never recertified on the current 0.5 bridge. Gen 5 is deprecated; left as is.
gate: Java 489 -> 491 passed (fresh); rebuilding HEAD unchanged reproduces 49CDB408. Pin test set (server versions, recapture identity, reference modes, entity profiles, contract publication, native world recordings, Region public benchmark): jar-16 baseline 77 passed + 10 errors (the old v6 drift); after the re-pin 13 more failed (9 recordings, 2 v8, 2 stamp) until the promotion, the re-freeze and the stamp; now back to the baseline (75 + the 12 publication tests, the same 10 v6 errors). tests/sim import order, fixture_blocks, live heard sounds 23 passed; adk test_bridge_client 6 passed.

## 2026-10-25 — skill_agent: walk_to's pace counts only the decisions inside each lane's horizon
command: the owner, 2026-10-25: "Fix pace to in-episode ticks, keep B opt-in". The trainer speed probe found that pace counted a lane's steady walk after its horizon, which the rollout still runs but nothing else scores.
result: Built by a Haiku subagent from a written spec, checked here.
- **`neural_skills/walk_to/skill.py`:** decision `t` counts toward pace only when `t < horizon_ticks`, the window the trainers' success and hold already use. One function serves every caller: walk_to's `metrics`, come_here's (through walk_to's), `teaching/certificates.py`'s E4 and `training/distill.py`'s walk pace. The walk_to README has a "Pace (E4)" row.
- **The saved modules, re-scored** (evaluation only, seed 77001, 256 lanes; the old definition reproduces every stored report value exactly). Within-5% share, module (steady lanes), bar 0.95:

| Module | Region | Old | New |
|---|---|---|---|
| V2 walk_to (6x500) | seen | 0.9777 (224) | 0.9777 (224) |
| V2 walk_to (6x500) | held out | 1.0000 (226) | 1.0000 (226) |
| come_here 6x2000 | seen | 0.8990 (208) | 0.8990 (208) |
| come_here 6x2000 | held out | 0.8621 (203) | 0.8663 (202) |
| come_here from walk_to | seen | 0.8877 (187) | 0.8877 (187) |
| come_here from walk_to | held out | 0.8607 (201) | 0.8650 (200) |

  No E4 verdict flips (24 rows scanned); the teacher rows are unchanged. Pace numbers in stored reports keep the old definition and are not rewritten.
- **Tests (local):** `test_pace_counts_only_the_decisions_inside_the_horizon` (a lane that stops at its horizon is within 5% only when in-episode decisions count). `test_sa_come_here.py`'s pinned walk_to evaluation digest moved 332077327b5cfed8 → cb15ef1471e0d967; with the old pace patched in, the old digest is reproduced, so the move is the pace alone.
gate: two passes, sharded, on 7a41487 with the pace fix, the trainer speed, the batched lowering and the path teacher: pass 1 the step-9 baseline's 11 failing names and one more, test_sa_gen7.py's launcher test, which failed only because the gate ran with HYTALEGYM_RENDER_CACHE set (since 7a41487 the launcher then mounts the cached render; the local test now clears it, and passes with and without it); the integration shard 7 passed; pass 2 1 failing name, a baseline one. On the rebased tree, its stack arm (V1 bodies, 32 held-out lanes, seed 77001) gives the same report batched and per agent in every non-timing field (combiner and bodies 21.5 -> 10.2 ms a tick), and test_sa_region_tasks.py and test_sa_path_teacher.py pass (14, after b12eec8's v8 re-freeze).
